- **recall-reflex**: Use at the START of any debug/build task or when you observe an abnormal/unforeseen condition (an error signature, a stacktrace, a surprising market/data state) — BEFORE reading code or acting. Also use when you just solved something non-obvious (bridge to capture). Pairs with the UserPromptSubmit recall hook + the recall() primitive.
